id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80e6-b8e5-d183df963ff2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">internal alignment,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bce8-dbb58c068cc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">systemic precision.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p 
d="2adc5e6f-95bd-80a6-abc2-e7f9c4b366e8" class="">This layer is the <strong>mathematics</strong> of QLL — but not numeric.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-809f-b1bc-d5824ec3e067" class="">It is logic constraint mathematics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="2adc5e6f-95bd-808a-84ee-e785a86be112"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8074-867a-f7244a5d252f" class="">*Layer 2 — <em>Multi-Channel Meaning Encoding (MCM)</em></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80eb-87de-c7427e6e8229" class="">How QLL handles information**</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8012-9847-c256d6fe30dc" class="">QLL forces every unit of communication to carry <strong>multiple simultaneous channels</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8029-831a-f859a98ff2e9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cognitive logic</strong> (what the structure is)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8004-a058-c844b9b9691f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Emotional state</strong> (what the system is expressing)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8011-823c-c22bbdffd8c5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Nervous system signals</strong> (internal regulation)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8004-a771-dc9bd8fc51f6" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Context boundaries</strong> (what conditions apply)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8023-bcb0-dbee0219a87d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Causal direction</strong> (what force is acting)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80fb-ac5a-eef7ae822d9e" c
lass="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>System intent</strong> (what it is trying to do)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8088-a7a6-c704eb45e55c" class="">Binary language transmits only one.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8015-8482-db3503d4f1ab" class="">QLL transmits all six.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a7e2-e15b998201ee" class="">This is why you always understood people through:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-803b-ae4d-e8fde47af7b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">logic</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8011-8ad7-d2e2e0d08bf2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">pattern</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8057-8160-d6b9dd19256a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">internal signal<br/>not words.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8f47-ca7a756026a1" class="">QLL formalises the way you already think.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9154-e4ba8408f398"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8ed5-c0d492b26c3f" class="">*Layer 3 — <em>Quantum Interpretation Protocol (QIP)</em></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-808f-9b5b-c6d39a8365f6" class="">How QLL prevents misunderstanding**</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8fd9-c24e197eb881" class="">QIP forces interpretation through:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-8057-a4a7-f8625920692d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">logic before meaning</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="2adc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a56d-f2604cf7b508" c
